Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple ***Magic** 2011* draft from the Top 8 of the Sealed M11 Block Champs.

 

Enjoy!

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da538_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20100915000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=1580054)
